Do not blindly comply with bots. Use when the user says "check PR &lt;N&gt; for stupid/valid comments", "triage PR &lt;N&gt;", or after a bot review (Copilot, CodeRabbit, claude-review, ChatGPT Codex) has posted comments. Returns a summary of what was fixed, what was rejected and why, and the resulting CI state. ([HoangNguyen0403/agent-skills-standard](https://github.com/HoangNguyen0403/agent-skills-standard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Runs the REVIEW.md passes (bugs, security, compliance) on the current branch's diff against main and reports findings ranked by severity with a tally line. Use before opening or updating a PR, once the verify gate is green. ([iengai/pbtb-rust](https://github.com/iengai/pbtb-rust/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: An autonomous agent that reviews pull requests with deep context awareness. ([imehr/vet](https://github.com/imehr/vet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Orchestrates the full PR review pipeline: fetch PRs from Bitbucket, review with deterministic pipeline + multi-persona analysis, merge findings, and optionally post comments. ([iqbal-mekari/claude-plugins](https://github.com/iqbal-mekari/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-review-agent/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Drives the no-mistakes validation gate for Angular, TypeScript, Nx monorepos, PrimeNG UI, and C#/.NET backends. Use this agent after workflow commits a feature branch and a no-mistakes gate run is required. ([j-mckerracher/agent-runner](https://github.com/j-mckerracher/agent-runner/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/12-pr-reviewer.agent.md) · 5 copies)
- **pr-reviewer**: Reviews PRs for correctness, verification quality, and merge readiness. ([JackSmack1971/CCSIF](https://github.com/JackSmack1971/CCSIF/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Reviews pull requests with comprehensive feedback. Checks code quality, tests, security, and convention compliance. For maintainers. ([jay05410/oss-claudecode](https://github.com/jay05410/oss-claudecode/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Reviews pull requests for code quality, security vulnerabilities, best practices compliance, and test coverage. Provides actionable feedback and approval recommendations. ([JoeyJoziah/investment-analysis-platform](https://github.com/JoeyJoziah/investment-analysis-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Reviews code changes for convention violations, bugs, security issues, missing i18n, and broken patterns in Repo PRs. ([johwer/marketplace](https://github.com/johwer/marketplace/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: WanSnapのPRをレビューする専門エージェント。AGENTS.mdのレビュー観点に加え、DDDの境界違反・認証認可漏れ・セキュリティ問題を重点的にチェックする。PRレビュー依頼時や自己レビュー時に使う。 ([KoshiFutami/wan-snap](https://github.com/KoshiFutami/wan-snap/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Narrow-mandate PR gate. Reviews diffs for reimplementation of existing capability, semantic naming mismatches, and acceptance-criteria coverage gaps. Runs after mechanical gates pass, before merge. ([kputegnat/firewalker](https://github.com/kputegnat/firewalker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: One council reviewer for Margot. Given exactly one card name and a pull request's facts, it reads that card from its own pr-council skill, fetches what the card names at the head sha, judges the diff against that card's standard under a refute posture, cites every finding at file:line, and returns that card's findings with a Checked block recording what it examined. It authors nothing, emits no verdict, and never sees another reviewer's findings. Spawned once per card by Margot, in its own fresh context, at the tier her card table names. ([lexijamesesq/publish-skills](https://github.com/lexijamesesq/publish-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: The merge gate. Reviews a pull request against its issue's acceptance criteria and the project invariants, reads CI for the gate result, mutation-tests the covering tests, and returns PASS / FAIL / BLOCKED. Use before every merge, and whenever asked whether a PR is ready. ([masudranam/autopilot](https://github.com/masudranam/autopilot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Senior developer-architect who reviews a prepared pull request end-to-end and gives the merge gate a verdict — APPROVE or REQUEST CHANGES. Reads the linked issue and its acceptance criteria, understands the changed code in context, and hunts for correctness bugs, security issues, weak spots, missing/way-too-thin tests, coverage gaps, blast-radius risks, and doc/changelog drift. Review-only: it never edits code — it posts a `gh pr review` with actionable findings and a clear ship/no-ship decision. Use before merging ANY prepared PR. ([mavrovde/beaconfolio](https://github.com/mavrovde/beaconfolio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Independent read-only code reviewer focused on correctness, security, maintainability, performance, scope discipline, and portfolio-quality Git narrative. ([MertArtun/orbital](https://github.com/MertArtun/orbital/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Independent code review after implementation. Eliminates self-review bias by reviewing changes the main session just wrote. v2 — mechanical auto-fix authority for local, risk_domain:none findings; security carve-out keyed on risk_domain. ([michaelhazza/sectool](https://github.com/michaelhazza/sectool/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Reviews a diff or pull request and reports findings ranked by severity, without touching the code. Use before committing, or when asked whether a change is safe to merge. Spawned only when something asks for a review; the solodev loop does not invoke it. ([mrfansi/solodev](https://github.com/mrfansi/solodev/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Top-tier review gate for conveyor-managed PRs. Spawn after a PR opens (give it the PR number); fix rounds return to the same reviewer (resume by name), spawned fresh only if it's gone. Reviews the full diff for correctness, repo law (CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md + project skills), gotcha regressions, scope, and test coverage. Clean → approved-by-agent label on PR + linked issue, no manufactured findings. Blocking → one inline review + card back to In Progress. Reviews only — never edits code or pushes. ([Nodman/conveyor](https://github.com/Nodman/conveyor/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Charter-format PR reviewer — the routine, Sonnet-tier reviewer on a PR. Spawn as the assigned roster reviewer for the second review slot. Sonnet minimum, NOT Haiku. Every PR must ALSO carry one merge-gate-reviewer (Opus) — see the safeguard in .claude/agents/README.md and .claude/team/charter/agents/orchestration-model.md § Model-tier selection when spawning. ([noorinalabs/noorinalabs-main](https://github.com/noorinalabs/noorinalabs-main/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Expert code reviewer that analyzes pull requests comprehensively. Reviews code quality, identifies bugs, assesses test coverage, evaluates documentation, and provides actionable feedback. Use when reviewing GitHub pull requests. ([olioapps/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/olioapps/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/git-actions/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Reviews local changes for convention compliance (CLAUDE.md), architecture integrity, and TDD adherence. Spawned automatically by the implement-task skill before pushing to GitHub. ([Outfitte/plugins](https://github.com/Outfitte/plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/implement-task/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Analysiert GitHub Pull Requests vollständig und dokumentiert das Review direkt via GitHub PR Review API. Prüft Code-Qualität, Konventionen, Tests und Requirements-Traceability (SDoc). Aufrufen mit "@pr-reviewer 217" oder "@pr-reviewer letzten offenen PR reviewen". ([paul-fleischmann-com/beaglebone_black](https://github.com/paul-fleischmann-com/beaglebone_black/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Reviews an open pull request across five axes (correctness, readability, architecture, security, performance), auto-fixes Critical and Important findings, and leaves Suggestions as review comments. Used by .github/workflows/pr-review.yml; adapted from addy-osmani/agent-skills' code-review-and-quality skill. Can also be invoked manually against a specific PR number. ([philjhale/order-system](https://github.com/philjhale/order-system/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: PR review and merge agent. Invoke when user mentions: review PR, merge PR, check PR, approve PR. Reviews code changes, checks for issues, and manages the merge workflow. Can also be used to review incoming PRs from other contributors. ([ProductoryHQ/ritemark-native](https://github.com/ProductoryHQ/ritemark-native/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: PR 审查员 — 对 PR diff 执行代码审查、分级修复、推送更新，回复与总结全程幂等。支持首轮审查和后续跟踪两种模式。 ([Room-C/ai-dev-workflow](https://github.com/Room-C/ai-dev-workflow/blob/HEAD/skills/review-pr/references/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: CodeRabbit-style PR Review Specialist. Takes a GitHub PR URL/number, produces a thorough CodeRabbit-style review (walkthrough, change summary table, per-file analysis, actionable inline comments with concrete code suggestions, nitpicks), presents it to the user for confirmation, then APPLIES approved suggestions, runs the quality suite, commits, and pushes. Unlike pr-manager (which addresses *existing reviewer comments*), pr-reviewer *generates* the CodeRabbit-style review itself. Use when the user says "review this PR", "do a coderabbit-style review of PR ([SUEYTAME/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/SUEYTAME/claude-skills-collection/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-agents/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Non-interactive read-only agent that reviews an external PR along one named dimension (correctness, conventions, concurrency), adversarially verifies a finding, or synthesizes surviving findings. Never edits or commits. Runs at the calling session's model. ([TadejOstanek/claude-workflow](https://github.com/TadejOstanek/claude-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Performs thorough code reviews with actionable feedback ([TonyCasey/agent-harness](https://github.com/TonyCasey/agent-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Reviews a pull request or the current branch diff before pushing. Checks against the story acceptance criteria, guards, and code quality. Pass a PR number or say "current branch". ([WANDERCOLTD/HF](https://github.com/WANDERCOLTD/HF/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer-acceptance**: Kata PR sub-reviewer for Definition of Done. Parses the linked GitHub issue's acceptance text (Kata uses an inline `**Acceptance:**` line or a `## Acceptance` section — both are supported) and cross-references each criterion against the diff. Posts ONE PR-level comment with ✅ Implemented / ❌ Missing / 🔲 Not yet evaluated / 💬 Notes sections. Invoked by /kata-pr-review. ([VicenzoMF/kata](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/kata/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-acceptance.md) · ★1)
- **pr-reviewer-acceptance**: Relay PR sub-reviewer for Definition of Done. Parses the linked GitHub issue's `## Acceptance` checklist and cross-references each criterion against the diff, posting ONE PR-level comment with ✅ Implemented / ❌ Missing / 🔲 Not yet evaluated / 💬 Notes sections. Invoked by /relay-pr-review. Relies on ADR 0007 (one PR per sub-issue) — every Relay PR has a Closes ([VicenzoMF/relay](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-acceptance.md))
- **pr-reviewer-agent**: PR Reviewer. Senior-level code review on every diff before commit. Reviews for: correctness, style consistency, security surface, performance, and test coverage. Use for: /review (code quality lens), pre-commit review on any language or framework. ([thecoderbuddy/agile-team-skill](https://github.com/thecoderbuddy/agile-team-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-agent.md) · ★9)
- **pr-reviewer-architecture**: Relay PR sub-reviewer (v2 inline pattern). Reads a diff and flags architectural / ADR violations that the static layer (lint, depcruise, archgate) cannot mechanically catch — judgment calls about VSA cohesion, ADR 0008 stack adherence, ADR 0009 error envelope misuse beyond the obvious c.json({error}) pattern, and "this belongs in shared/, not in the slice". Invoked by /relay-pr-review. Posts inline comments via gh api with marker &lt;!-- relay-review:architecture --&gt;. ([VicenzoMF/relay](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-architecture.md))
- **pr-reviewer-consolidator**: Kata PR review consolidator. Runs LAST, after the parallel sub-reviewers. Fetches all &lt;!-- kata-review:* --&gt; inline + PR-level comments via gh api, deduplicates by {path, line} ±3, groups by severity, detects files with zero coverage from any inline agent, and posts ONE PR-level summary comment with metadata table, counts, highlights, and gap detection. Invoked by /kata-pr-review skill. ([VicenzoMF/kata](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/kata/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-consolidator.md) · ★1)
- **pr-reviewer-consolidator**: Relay PR review consolidator. Runs LAST, after the eight sub-reviewers. Fetches all &lt;!-- relay-review:* --&gt; inline + PR-level comments via gh api, deduplicates by {path, line} ±3, groups by severity, detects files with zero coverage from any agent, and posts ONE PR-level summary comment with metadata table, counts, highlights, and gap detection. Invoked by /relay-pr-review skill. ([VicenzoMF/relay](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-consolidator.md))
- **pr-reviewer-conventions**: Relay PR sub-reviewer (v2 inline pattern). Reads a diff and flags violations of docs/conventions.md and docs/conventions-backend.md that the static layer doesn't catch — pino vs console, env.ts vs scattered process.env, named exports vs default, kebab-case filenames vs camelCase, Zod-as-single-source-of-truth misuse, slice composition root wiring, .js suffix in TS relative imports. Invoked by /relay-pr-review. Posts inline comments via gh api with marker &lt;!-- relay-review:conventions --&gt;. ([VicenzoMF/relay](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-conventions.md))
- **pr-reviewer-correctness**: Reviews a pull request diff for correctness defects - bugs, unhandled errors, edge cases, concurrency, data loss, and security. Reports only findings with a concrete failure scenario. Dispatched by the review-pr skill alongside pr-reviewer-design. Read-only - never edits files. ([konvoulgaris/soong](https://github.com/konvoulgaris/soong/blob/HEAD/plugins/soong/agents/pr-reviewer-correctness.md) · ★2)
- **pr-reviewer-design**: Reviews a pull request diff for contract and structural defects - API and schema breakage, misleading boundaries, and missing coverage on risky paths. Reports only findings with a concrete consequence. Dispatched by the review-pr skill alongside pr-reviewer-correctness. Read-only - never edits files. ([konvoulgaris/soong](https://github.com/konvoulgaris/soong/blob/HEAD/plugins/soong/agents/pr-reviewer-design.md) · ★2)
- **pr-reviewer-expert**: PR review agent crystallized from reverse-engineering CodeRabbit. Consult when reviewing PRs, checking diffs for bugs/security/performance, or when the user asks to review changes before committing or pushing. Trigger conditions: git diff output, PR descriptions, "review this", "check these changes", pre-push review, post-implementation quality check. Reads the full codebase context - not just the diff - to catch cross-file issues that line-by-line review misses. &lt;example&gt; Context: User just completed a cycle and wants to review quality. user: "Review this PR" assistant: "Let me check the dif… ([drobins25/craft](https://github.com/drobins25/craft/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer-expert.md) · ★68)
- **pr-reviewer-instructions**: This file's text is included verbatim in the prompt of every commissioned PR reviewer. The instruments that commission reviewers read it at composition time and paste it in — never point at it, never paraphrase it. (Ruled 2026-08-30: one copy, included everywhere; a pointed-at rule gets skipped, a paraphrased one drifts.) ([nedschorus/nedschorus](https://github.com/nedschorus/nedschorus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/pr-reviewer-instructions.md) · ★1)
- **pr-reviewer-performance**: Relay PR sub-reviewer for clearly-visible performance issues. Flags blocking I/O on hot paths, unbounded loops over user input, sequential await where Promise.all fits, fetch without abort/timeout, repeated work that should be memoized, and (when Relay grows a DB in M1+) N+1 queries and missing transactions. Only flags issues clearly visible in the diff — no speculation. Invoked by /relay-pr-review. ([VicenzoMF/relay](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-performance.md))
- **pr-reviewer-regression**: Kata PR sub-reviewer for AI-generated-code regressions and hallucinations. Catches unrelated deletions, phantom imports, wrong method signatures, weakened test assertions, dead code, TODOs in production, type-cast bypasses (the `any` ban from AGENTS.md is the headline rule), and silently swallowed errors. Kata code is largely AI-written so this is high-value. Invoked by /kata-pr-review. Posts inline comments via gh api. ([VicenzoMF/kata](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/kata/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-regression.md) · ★1)
- **pr-reviewer-regression**: Relay PR sub-reviewer for AI-generated-code regressions and hallucinations. Catches unrelated deletions, phantom imports, wrong method signatures, weakened test assertions, dead code, TODOs in production, type-cast bypasses, and silently swallowed errors. Relay code is largely AI-written (via /relay-feature-dev) so this is high-value. Invoked by /relay-pr-review. Posts inline comments via gh api. ([VicenzoMF/relay](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-regression.md))
- **pr-reviewer-security**: Relay PR sub-reviewer (v2 inline pattern). Reads a diff and flags security-relevant patterns that gitleaks (plain-text secrets) cannot — shell injection in child_process spawn/exec, SSRF in fetch URLs built from inputs, regex DoS, missing auth on new routes, prototype pollution, unsafe deserialization, log leakage of PII/tokens, hardcoded credentials shaped like config. Invoked by /relay-pr-review. Posts inline comments via gh api with marker &lt;!-- relay-review:security --&gt;. ([VicenzoMF/relay](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-security.md))
- **pr-reviewer-summary**: Kata PR sub-reviewer. Writes a TL;DR paragraph + "what this enables" bullets + (conditionally) a Mermaid diagram of new request flow, all posted as ONE PR-level comment (not inline). Invoked first by the /kata-pr-review skill so the consolidator can pin the summary at the top. Mermaid is conditional — emitted only when the PR introduces a new route, middleware, or scoped-slot flow. ([VicenzoMF/kata](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/kata/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-summary.md) · ★1)
- **pr-reviewer-summary**: Relay PR sub-reviewer. Writes a TL;DR paragraph + "what this enables" bullets + (conditionally) a Mermaid diagram of the new flow, all posted as ONE PR-level comment (not inline). Invoked first by the /relay-pr-review skill so the consolidator can pin the summary at the top. Mermaid is conditional — emitted only when the PR introduces new request flow / state / multi-service interaction. ([VicenzoMF/relay](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-summary.md))
- **pr-reviewer-tests**: Relay PR sub-reviewer (v2 inline pattern). Reads a diff and flags test-coverage gaps per Relay's "testing strategy is deferred but skeletons land with the slice" rule — new HTTP slice without test/api/&lt;slice&gt;.hurl (ADR 0016), new handler without handler.test.ts stub, MCP tool changes without a matching verify_mcp_tool fixture, error paths visible in code with no corresponding assertion, weakened test assertions. Invoked by /relay-pr-review. Posts inline comments via gh api with marker &lt;!-- relay-review:tests --&gt;. ([VicenzoMF/relay](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-tests.md))
- **pr-reviewer.README**: &gt; Part of the [`claude-code-toolkit`](../../../README.md) `/prp-orchestrate` agent team · [← back to root README](../../../README.md#agents) · definition: [`pr-reviewer.md`](./pr-reviewer.md) ([arturgomes/claude-code-toolkit](https://github.com/arturgomes/claude-code-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/codebase-intelligence/agents/pr-reviewer.README.md) · ★1)
- **pr-risk-reviewer**: Advisory PR risk review aid. Checks Tier C path touches, new external dependencies, schema changes, test coverage delta, and scope bleed. Returns RISK: LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH with specific reasons for the orchestrator; GitHub checks, Merge Gate, and PM policy remain the blocking authority. ([griff843/Unit-Talk-v2](https://github.com/griff843/Unit-Talk-v2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-risk-reviewer.md))
- **pr-scope-reviewer**: Use proactively before opening or updating a PR — after staging changes, before `gh pr create`, or whenever the user asks to check a branch's scope. Diffs the working branch against main and flags scope creep: unrelated files, stacked-branch drift, stray migrations, package.json/lockfile pollution, CopilotKit version drift, and accidental refactors that bloat the PR past its stated intent. Read-only — never modifies, commits, or pushes. Output is a scope verdict table the human acts on. ([amo-tech-ai/mdeapp](https://github.com/amo-tech-ai/mdeapp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-scope-reviewer.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **pr-screenshots**: For any PR that changes a navigable view, attach before/after screenshots so the reviewer (human or `/review` persona) sees the actual visual result, not just the diff. The repo already has the capture tool — this is the convention for using it. ([sentinel-web/community-manager](https://github.com/sentinel-web/community-manager/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/pr-screenshots.md) · ★1)
- **pr-screenshotter**: Produces real before/after PNG screenshots for a YAS branch's rendering changes and publishes them to the yas-pr-screenshots repo, returning a ready-to-paste markdown before/after table. Delegate to this agent for "screenshot this branch", "shoot before/after images for the PR", "capture statusline screenshots", or when a PR-authoring flow needs image screenshots (not ANSI text) for the Screenshots section. It picks the relevant demo scenarios from the diff, renders both sides, commits + pushes to the screenshots repo's main, and hands back ONLY the table — it does NOT edit the PR body (that'… ([tmck-code/yet-another-statusline](https://github.com/tmck-code/yet-another-statusline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-screenshotter.md) · ★242)
- **pr-scribe**: Write the body for ONE pull request and open or update it. Spawn this whenever a PR needs a body — the isolated context is the point: with nothing else in scope it must read the real diff and the real issue, so it describes what actually landed instead of narrating the work from memory. Input: the repo, the branch, and the issue number (or the acceptance criteria inlined). Returns the PR URL. ([DYB-Development/symphony](https://github.com/DYB-Development/symphony/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-scribe.md))
- **pr-security**: PR 보안 검증 에이전트. /security 명령어 또는 PR 생성/업데이트 시 보안 취약점을 분석하고 잠재적 보안 이슈를 식별합니다. ([dding-g/ddingg-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/dding-g/ddingg-claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/pr-agents/agents/pr-security.md))
- **pr-security-backstop**: Read-only independent Security/Bugs backstop for litmus PR mode. Dispatched alongside the Codex lead reviewer to provide cross-model diversity on the two highest-risk lenses. Emits a strict confidence-bearing JSON verdict and NEVER modifies files. Used only by the litmus pre-PR gate, never invoked directly. ([chris-yyau/busdriver](https://github.com/chris-yyau/busdriver/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-security-backstop.md) · ★1)
- **pr-security-review**: Use this agent to perform a security analysis of a pull request BEFORE checking out or running any code locally. Analyzes diffs and changed files for malware, dangerous actions, supply chain attacks, credential theft, and other security risks. Returns a verdict of SAFE, REVIEW NEEDED, or BLOCK. Must be run before any local checkout or test execution. ([prime-radiant-inc/github-triage](https://github.com/prime-radiant-inc/github-triage/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-security-review.md) · ★4)
- **pr-security-reviewer**: Review a PR through a SECURITY lens — trace how every sensitive value flows from write to every reader, plus data exposure, deletion safety, credential surfaces, and injection. Read-only — never writes or edits. Reports issues with file:line citations and severity. ([go-to-k/cdkd](https://github.com/go-to-k/cdkd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-security-reviewer.md) · ★139)
- **pr-security-reviewer**: Comprehensive pre-merge security agent — parallel SAST + SCA + secrets + container + IaC + license against the PR diff, dep-add-guard for new direct deps, optional `gh pr review` posting. ([Vulnetix/pix-ai-coding-assistant](https://github.com/Vulnetix/pix-ai-coding-assistant/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/agents/pr-security-reviewer.md) · ★9)
- **pr-security-reviewer**: Comprehensive pre-merge security agent — parallel SAST + SCA + secrets + container + IaC + license against the PR diff, dep-add-guard for new direct deps, optional `gh pr review` posting.
